    const RTL_LANGS = new Set(["fa", "ar", "ps", "ur"]);

    function esc(s) {
      return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","\"":"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));
    }

    function t(key, vars) {
      const pack = I18N[state.lang] || I18N.en;
      let s = (pack && pack[key] != null) ? pack[key] : (I18N.en[key] != null ? I18N.en[key] : key);
      if (vars) {
        Object.keys(vars).forEach(k => { s = s.split("{" + k + "}").join(String(vars[k])); });
      }
      return s;
    }

    function assetName(a) {
      const map = ASSET_I18N[state.lang];
      const base = map && map[a.code];
      if (!base) return a.name;
      const m = a.name.match(/\s*(\([^)]+\))$/);
      return m ? base + " " + m[1] : base;
    }

    function ledgerNote(item) {
      if (item.noteKey === "ledgerSent") return t("ledgerSent", { who: item.who, reason: t(item.reasonKey) });
      if (item.noteKey === "ledgerRequested") return t("ledgerRequested", { who: item.who, reason: t(item.reasonKey) });
      if (item.noteKey === "ledgerSupply") return t("ledgerSupply", { title: t(item.titleKey) });
      if (item.noteKey === "ledgerBuy") return t("ledgerBuy", { code: item.code });
      if (item.noteKey === "ledgerSell") return t("ledgerSell", { code: item.code });
      if (item.noteKey) return t(item.noteKey);
      return item.note || "";
    }

    function ledgerTime(item) {
      return item.timeKey ? t(item.timeKey) : (item.time || "");
    }

    function renderRecipientOptions() {
      const sel = document.getElementById("sendRecipientSelect");
      if (!sel) return;
      const current = sel.value;
      sel.innerHTML = state.contacts.map(c => {
        const value = c.name + " (" + c.handle + ")";
        const rel = c.relationKey ? t(c.relationKey) : (c.relation || "");
        return '<option value="' + esc(value) + '">' + esc(c.name) + " (" + esc(c.handle) + ") — " + esc(rel) + "</option>";
      }).join("");
      if ([...sel.options].some(o => o.value === current)) sel.value = current;
    }

    function renderSocialOptions() {
      const sel = document.getElementById("socialPlatformSelect");
      if (!sel) return;
      const current = sel.value;
      const opts = [["X (@BidakBank)","optX"],["Facebook Official","optFb"],["YouTube (@BidakBank)","optYt"],["WeChat (BidakBank)","optWc"]];
      sel.innerHTML = opts.map(([v, k]) => '<option value="' + v + '">' + esc(t(k)) + "</option>").join("");
      if ([...sel.options].some(o => o.value === current)) sel.value = current;
    }

    function applyStaticI18n() {
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n]").forEach(el => { el.textContent = t(el.getAttribute("data-i18n")); });
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n-html]").forEach(el => { el.innerHTML = t(el.getAttribute("data-i18n-html")); });
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n-placeholder]").forEach(el => { el.placeholder = t(el.getAttribute("data-i18n-placeholder")); });
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n-aria]").forEach(el => { el.setAttribute("aria-label", t(el.getAttribute("data-i18n-aria"))); });
      const role = document.getElementById("headerRoleTag");
      if (role && state.roleKey) role.textContent = t(state.roleKey);
      const sel = document.getElementById("languageSelect");
      if (sel) sel.value = state.lang;
      const badge = document.getElementById("langDirBadge");
      if (badge) badge.textContent = RTL_LANGS.has(state.lang) ? "RTL" : "LTR";
      document.title = "BidakBank (@BidakBank) — " + t("docTitle");
      renderRecipientOptions();
      renderSocialOptions();
    }

    function setLanguage(code, announce) {
      if (!I18N[code]) code = "en";
      state.lang = code;
      document.documentElement.lang = code === "zh" ? "zh-Hans" : code;
      document.documentElement.dir = RTL_LANGS.has(code) ? "rtl" : "ltr";
      try { localStorage.setItem("bidak_lang", code); } catch (e) {}
      applyStaticI18n();
      renderStories();
      renderContacts();
      renderSupplyPackages();
      renderAssets();
      renderLiquidityFeed();
      renderBanks();
      renderLedger();
      const modal = document.getElementById("sendModal");
      if (modal && !modal.classList.contains("hidden")) openSendModal(state.transferMode);
      const story = document.getElementById("storyModal");
      if (story && !story.classList.contains("hidden") && state.openStoryIdx != null) openStory(state.openStoryIdx);
      if (announce) showToast(t("toastLang"));
    }

    // ==================== CORE HELPERS ====================
    function showToast(msg) {
      const box = document.getElementById('toastBox');
      box.textContent = msg;
      box.classList.remove('hidden');
      setTimeout(() => box.classList.add('hidden'), 3200);
    }

    function updateBalancesUI() {
      const freeBdk = Math.max(0, state.balanceBdk - state.socialScore);
      document.getElementById('walletBalanceBdk').textContent = state.balanceBdk.toLocaleString('en-US');
      document.getElementById('walletBalanceBdki').textContent = (state.balanceBdk / 100).toFixed(2);
      document.getElementById('freeLiquidBdk').textContent = `${freeBdk.toLocaleString('en-US')} BDK`;
      document.getElementById('freeLiquidBdki').textContent = `${(freeBdk / 100).toFixed(2)} BDKi`;

      document.getElementById('socialScoreVal').textContent = state.socialScore;
      document.getElementById('floorBdkDisplay').textContent = `${state.socialScore} BDK`;
      document.getElementById('floorBdkiDisplay').textContent = (state.socialScore / 100).toFixed(2);
      document.getElementById('socialScoreBar').style.width = `${Math.min(100, (state.socialScore / 1000) * 100)}%`;
      document.getElementById('dailyUsedBdki').textContent = state.dailyUsedBdki.toFixed(2);
    }

    // ==================== RENDER FUNCTIONS ====================
    function renderStories() {
      const bar = document.getElementById('storyBar');
      bar.innerHTML = state.stories.map((s, idx) => `
        <button onclick="openStory(${idx})" class="flex flex-col items-center shrink-0 w-16 group">
          <div class="${s.type === 'History' ? 'story-ring' : 'story-ring-insight'} rounded-full">
            <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-[#061812] flex items-center justify-center text-xl group-hover:scale-105 transition">
              ${s.emoji}
            </div>
          </div>
          <span class="text-[10px] text-emerald-100 truncate w-full text-center mt-1.5 font-medium">${esc(t(s.shortKey))}</span>
          <span class="text-[9px] px-1.5 rounded ${s.type === 'History' ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300'}">${esc(t(s.type === 'History' ? 'typeHistory' : 'typeInsight'))}</span>
        </button>
      `).join('');
    }

    function renderContacts() {
      const list = document.getElementById('contactsList');
      list.innerHTML = state.contacts.map((c, idx) => `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-black/35 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${esc(c.name)}</span>
              <span class="text-[10px] font-mono-num text-amber-300">${esc(c.handle)}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-300/75 mt-0.5">${esc(t(c.relationKey))} • ${esc(t('scoreWord'))}: ${c.score} • <span class="text-emerald-400">${esc(t(c.statusKey))}</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
            <button onclick="openSendModal('send', ${idx})" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-500 text-[#03150e] font-bold text-[11px]">${esc(t('btnSend'))}</button>
            <button onclick="openSendModal('request', ${idx})" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white/10 text-emerald-200 font-semibold text-[11px]">${esc(t('btnReq'))}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function renderSupplyPackages() {
      const grid = document.getElementById('supplyPackagesGrid');
      grid.innerHTML = state.supplyPackages.map(pkg => `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-black/45 border border-amber-400/25 flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <div class="text-xl">${pkg.icon}</div>
            <div class="text-xs font-bold text-white mt-1">${esc(t(pkg.titleKey))}</div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-300/70 mt-0.5">${esc(t(pkg.destKey))}</div>
          </div>
          <button onclick="orderFamilySupply('${pkg.id}')" class="mt-2.5 w-full py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-400/20 hover:bg-amber-400/30 border border-amber-400/40 text-amber-300 font-mono-num font-bold text-[11px]">
            ${esc(t('btnSend'))} • ${pkg.costBdk} BDK
          </button>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function renderAssets() {
      const q = document.getElementById('assetSearchInput').value.toLowerCase();
      const filtered = state.assets.filter(a => {
        const matchPillar = state.pillarFilter === 'all' || a.pillar === state.pillarFilter;
        const matchType = state.typeFilter === 'all' || a.type === state.typeFilter;
        const label = assetName(a).toLowerCase();
        const matchQuery = !q || a.code.toLowerCase().includes(q) || a.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || label.includes(q);
        return matchPillar && matchType && matchQuery;
      });

      const pillarBadges = {
        anchors: 'pillarAnchors',
        kinship: 'pillarKinship',
        trade: 'pillarTrade',
        real: 'pillarReal'
      };

      document.getElementById('assetsGrid').innerHTML = filtered.map(a => {
        const held = state.holdings[a.code] || 0;
        return `
          <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-black/40 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="text-xs font-extrabold font-mono-num text-white">${a.code}</span>
                <span class="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/5 text-emerald-300">${esc(t(pillarBadges[a.pillar]))}</span>
                <span class="text-[9px] uppercase px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ${a.type === 'fiat' ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300'}">${esc(t(a.type === 'fiat' ? 'wordFiat' : 'wordDigital'))}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="text-[11px] text-emerald-200/80 mt-0.5">${esc(assetName(a))}</div>
              <div class="text-[10px] font-mono-num text-amber-300/90 mt-0.5">${esc(t('rateWord'))}: ${a.rateBdk.toFixed(2)} BDK • ${esc(t('heldWord'))}: ${held}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
              <button onclick="tradeAsset('${a.code}', ${a.rateBdk}, 1)" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-400 text-[#03150e] font-bold text-xs">${esc(t('btnBuy'))}</button>
              <button onclick="tradeAsset('${a.code}', ${a.rateBdk}, -1)" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white font-semibold text-xs">${esc(t('btnSell'))}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    function renderLiquidityFeed() {
      const container = document.getElementById('liquidityFeedContainer');
      container.innerHTML = state.providers.map((p, idx) => `
        <div class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-black/45 border border-emerald-500/25 space-y-2.5">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <div class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${p.name}</div>
              <div class="text-[11px] text-emerald-400">${p.corridor}</div>
            </div>
            <span class="text-[10px] font-mono-num px-2 py-1 rounded bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/30">${esc(t(p.rateKey))}</span>
          </div>
          <button onclick="showToast(t('videoPlay'))" class="w-full py-6 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-950 via-teal-900/70 to-emerald-950 border border-emerald-400/30 text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:border-amber-400 transition">
            ${esc(t(p.videoKey))}
          </button>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-emerald-200/80 bg-white/5 p-2 rounded-lg">${esc(t(p.reviewKey))}</div>
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
            <button onclick="upvoteProvider(${idx})" class="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-500/20 hover:bg-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 text-[11px] font-bold">
              ${esc(t('verifiedFast'))} (${p.upvotes})
            </button>
            <button onclick="openSendModal('send')" class="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-400 text-black text-[11px] font-bold">
              ${esc(t('useCorridor'))}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function renderBanks() {
      const board = document.getElementById('banksLeaderboard');
      board.innerHTML = state.banks.map((b, idx) => `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-xl bg-black/40 border ${b.aligned ? 'border-emerald-500/35' : 'border-rose-500/40'} flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${b.aligned ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-500'}"></span>
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">${esc(b.name)}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] mt-1 ${b.aligned ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-rose-300'}">
              ${esc(t('coopLine'))} <strong class="font-mono-num">${b.score}/100</strong> • ${esc(t(b.aligned ? 'aligned' : 'restrictive'))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onclick="downgradeBank(${idx})" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-500/20 hover:bg-rose-500/30 border border-rose-400/40 text-rose-200 text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0">
            ${esc(t('reportFriction'))}
          </button>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function renderLedger() {
      document.getElementById('ledgerHistoryList').innerHTML = state.ledger.map(item => `
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-xs p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-black/35 border border-white/5">
          <div>
            <div class="font-semibold text-white">${esc(ledgerNote(item))}</div>
            <div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-400/70">${esc(ledgerTime(item))}</div>
          </div>
          <span class="font-mono-num font-bold ${item.type === 'income' ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}">
            ${item.type === 'income' ? '+' : '-'}${item.amount} BDK
          </span>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    // ==================== INTERACTIONS ====================
    function switchTab(tabName) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-view').forEach(el => el.classList.add('hidden'));
      document.getElementById(`view-${tabName}`).classList.remove('hidden');
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('tab-active'));
      document.getElementById(`tab-${tabName}`).classList.add('tab-active');
    }

    function selectKinshipBadge(badge) {
      state.selectedBadge = badge;
      document.querySelectorAll('.kinship-badge').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('bg-emerald-500/20', 'border-emerald-400');
        b.classList.add('bg-black/30', 'border-emerald-500/20');
      });
      const active = document.getElementById(`badge-${badge}`);
      active.classList.remove('bg-black/30', 'border-emerald-500/20');
      active.classList.add('bg-emerald-500/20', 'border-emerald-400');
      const badgeName = { servant: 'badgeServant', maternal: 'badgeMaternal', paternal: 'badgePaternal' };
      showToast(t('toastBadge', { role: t(badgeName[badge] || 'badgeServant') }));
    }

    function openSendModal(mode, prefillRecipient = null) {
      state.transferMode = mode;
      document.getElementById('sendModalTitle').textContent = mode === 'send' ? t('sendModalTitle') : t('requestModalTitle');
      renderRecipientOptions();
      if (prefillRecipient !== null && prefillRecipient !== undefined) {
        const sel = document.getElementById('sendRecipientSelect');
        const contact = typeof prefillRecipient === 'number' ? state.contacts[prefillRecipient] : null;
        const needle = contact ? contact.name.split(' ')[0] : String(prefillRecipient).split(' ')[0];
        for (let opt of sel.options) {
          if (opt.value.includes(needle)) sel.value = opt.value;
        }
      }
      const pills = document.getElementById('reasonPills');
      pills.innerHTML = state.reasons.map(r => `
        <button type="button" onclick="selectReason('${r}')" class="reason-pill p-2 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-semibold text-left border transition ${state.selectedReason === r ? 'bg-amber-400/25 border-amber-400 text-amber-200' : 'bg-black/40 border-white/10 text-emerald-200'}">
          ${esc(t(r))}
        </button>
      `).join('');
      document.getElementById('sendModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeSendModal() {
      document.getElementById('sendModal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function selectReason(reason) {
      state.selectedReason = reason;
      openSendModal(state.transferMode);
    }

    function executeTransfer() {
      const amt = Math.round(Number(document.getElementById('sendAmountInput').value));
      const recipient = document.getElementById('sendRecipientSelect').value;
      if (!amt || amt < 1) {
        showToast(t('toastMinUnit'));
        return;
      }

      if (state.transferMode === 'send') {
        if (state.balanceBdk - amt < state.socialScore) {
          showToast(t('toastFloor', { score: state.socialScore }));
          return;
        }
        const amtBdki = amt / 100;
        if (state.dailyUsedBdki + amtBdki > 2345.67) {
          showToast(t('toastDaily'));
          return;
        }
        state.balanceBdk -= amt;
        state.dailyUsedBdki += amtBdki;
        state.ledger.unshift({ type: 'expense', noteKey: 'ledgerSent', who: recipient.split(' ')[0], reasonKey: state.selectedReason, amount: amt, timeKey: 'timeNow' });
        showToast(t('toastSent', { amt, bdki: amtBdki.toFixed(2), reason: t(state.selectedReason) }));
      } else {
        state.balanceBdk += amt;
        state.ledger.unshift({ type: 'income', noteKey: 'ledgerRequested', who: recipient.split(' ')[0], reasonKey: state.selectedReason, amount: amt, timeKey: 'timeNow' });
        showToast(t('toastReceived', { amt, reason: t(state.selectedReason) }));
      }

      updateBalancesUI();
      renderLedger();
      closeSendModal();
    }

    function orderFamilySupply(id) {
      const pkg = state.supplyPackages.find(p => p.id === id);
      if (!pkg) return;
      const costBdk = pkg.costBdk;
      if (state.balanceBdk - costBdk < state.socialScore) {
        showToast(t('toastFloorShort', { score: state.socialScore }));
        return;
      }
      state.balanceBdk -= costBdk;
      state.dailyUsedBdki += costBdk / 100;
      state.ledger.unshift({ type: 'expense', noteKey: 'ledgerSupply', titleKey: pkg.titleKey, amount: costBdk, timeKey: 'timeNow' });
      updateBalancesUI();
      renderLedger();
      showToast(t('toastSupply', { title: t(pkg.titleKey), cost: costBdk }));
    }

    function tradeAsset(code, rateBdk, dir) {
      const cost = Math.max(1, Math.round(rateBdk));
      if (dir === 1) {
        if (state.balanceBdk - cost < state.socialScore) {
          showToast(t('toastKeepLiquid', { score: state.socialScore }));
          return;
        }
        state.balanceBdk -= cost;
        state.holdings[code] = (state.holdings[code] || 0) + 1;
        state.ledger.unshift({ type: 'expense', noteKey: 'ledgerBuy', code, amount: cost, timeKey: 'timeNow' });
        showToast(t('toastAcquired', { code, cost }));
      } else {
        if (!state.holdings[code] || state.holdings[code] <= 0) {
          showToast(t('toastNoHold', { code }));
          return;
        }
        state.holdings[code] -= 1;
        state.balanceBdk += cost;
        state.ledger.unshift({ type: 'income', noteKey: 'ledgerSell', code, amount: cost, timeKey: 'timeNow' });
        showToast(t('toastSold', { code, cost }));
      }
      updateBalancesUI();
      renderAssets();
      renderLedger();
    }

    function setPillarFilter(p) {
      state.pillarFilter = p;
      document.querySelectorAll('.pillar-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('bg-emerald-500', 'text-[#03150e]', 'font-bold');
        b.classList.add('bg-white/5', 'text-emerald-200');
      });
      const active = document.getElementById(`pillar-${p}`);
      active.classList.remove('bg-white/5', 'text-emerald-200');
      active.classList.add('bg-emerald-500', 'text-[#03150e]', 'font-bold');
      renderAssets();
    }

    function setTypeFilter(t) {
      state.typeFilter = t;
      document.querySelectorAll('.type-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('bg-emerald-500/25', 'border-emerald-400', 'text-white');
        b.classList.add('bg-black/30', 'border-emerald-500/20', 'text-emerald-300');
      });
      const active = document.getElementById(`type-${t}`);
      active.classList.remove('bg-black/30', 'border-emerald-500/20', 'text-emerald-300');
      active.classList.add('bg-emerald-500/25', 'border-emerald-400', 'text-white');
      renderAssets();
    }

    function boostSocialScore() {
      if (state.socialScore >= 1000) return;
      state.socialScore = Math.min(1000, state.socialScore + 15);
      if (state.balanceBdk < state.socialScore) {
        state.balanceBdk = state.socialScore;
      }
      updateBalancesUI();
      showToast(t('toastPeer', { score: state.socialScore }));
    }

    function upvoteProvider(idx) {
      state.providers[idx].upvotes += 1;
      renderLiquidityFeed();
      showToast(t('toastUpvote'));
    }

    function downgradeBank(idx) {
      state.banks[idx].score = Math.max(5, state.banks[idx].score - 12);
      state.banks[idx].aligned = state.banks[idx].score >= 60;
      renderBanks();
      showToast(t('toastFriction', { name: state.banks[idx].name, score: state.banks[idx].score }));
    }

    function migrateToVault() {
      showToast(t('toastVault'));
    }

    function addLedgerEntry(type) {
      const typed = document.getElementById('ledgerNoteInput').value.trim();
      const amt = Math.round(Number(document.getElementById('ledgerAmountInput').value));
      if (!amt || amt < 1) {
        showToast(t('toastMinLedger'));
        return;
      }
      if (type === 'expense' && state.balanceBdk - amt < state.socialScore) {
        showToast(t('toastFloorLedger', { score: state.socialScore }));
        return;
      }
      state.balanceBdk += type === 'income' ? amt : -amt;
      const entry = { type, amount: amt, timeKey: 'timeNow' };
      if (typed) entry.note = typed;
      else entry.noteKey = type === 'income' ? 'ledgerSkill' : 'ledgerHousehold';
      state.ledger.unshift(entry);
      document.getElementById('ledgerNoteInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('ledgerAmountInput').value = '';
      updateBalancesUI();
      renderLedger();
      showToast(t('toastLogged', { sign: type === 'income' ? '+' : '-', amt }));
    }

    function addTrustedContactPrompt() {
      const name = prompt(t('toastPrompt'));
      if (!name) return;
      const handle = '@' + name.replace(/\s+/g, '');
      state.contacts.unshift({ name, handle, relationKey: 'relAdded', score: 850, statusKey: 'statusDemand' });
      renderContacts();
      showToast(t('toastAdded', { name }));
    }

    function openStory(idx) {
      state.openStoryIdx = idx;
      const s = state.stories[idx];
      document.getElementById('storyBadgeType').textContent = t(s.type === 'History' ? 'typeHistory' : 'typeInsight');
      document.getElementById('storyEmoji').textContent = s.emoji;
      document.getElementById('storyTitle').textContent = t(s.titleKey);
      document.getElementById('storyBody').textContent = t(s.bodyKey);
      document.getElementById('storyModal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeStoryModal() {
      document.getElementById('storyModal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function toggleDrawer(open) {
      document.getElementById('profileDrawer').classList.toggle('hidden', !open);
    }

    function setChessAvatar(symbol, roleKey) {
      state.roleKey = roleKey;
      document.getElementById('headerAvatarCircle').textContent = symbol;
      document.getElementById('headerRoleTag').textContent = t(roleKey);
      showToast(t('toastAvatar', { role: t(roleKey) }));
    }

    function verifyAndClaimBonus() {
      const name = document.getElementById('profileNameInput').value.trim();
      const handle = document.getElementById('profileHandleInput').value.trim();
      const bankName = document.getElementById('bankLegalNameInput').value.trim();
      if (!name || !bankName || name.toLowerCase() !== bankName.toLowerCase()) {
        showToast(t('toastMismatch'));
        return;
      }
      document.getElementById('headerFullName').textContent = name;
      document.getElementById('headerHandle').textContent = handle.startsWith('@') ? handle : '@' + handle;

      const claimKey = `bidak_claimed_${name.toLowerCase()}`;
      if (localStorage.getItem(claimKey)) {
        showToast(t('toastVerifiedAlready'));
      } else {
        localStorage.setItem(claimKey, 'true');
        showToast(t('toastVerifiedNew'));
      }
      toggleDrawer(false);
    }

    function copyInviteMessage() {
      const text = t('inviteText');
      navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text);
      showToast(t('toastInvite'));
    }

    function copyWeChat() {
      navigator.clipboard?.writeText('BidakBank');
      showToast(t('toastWechat'));
    }

    // ==================== INIT ====================
    let savedLang = 'en';
    try { savedLang = localStorage.getItem('bidak_lang') || 'en'; } catch (e) {}
    setLanguage(I18N[savedLang] ? savedLang : 'en', false);
  </script>
</body>
</html>
